# Skills · T · part 53

845 entries, thesis-tex-writeback to thinking-in-systems. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **thesis-tex-writeback**: 把已经在 Markdown 中间层收敛的中文毕业论文内容回写到 `chapters/*.tex` 与相关交付层文件，保持结构、图表、公式、交叉引用与章节承接一致。 **Trigger**: tex 回写, thesis tex writeback, md 回 tex, 章节回写, 论文交付层同步. **Use when**: 章节主线、术语、符号和图表计划已经在 Markdown 层基本稳定，需要进入 TeX 交付层。 **Skip if**: 结构还没稳定，或者仍在章级重构阶段。 **Network**: none. **Guardrail**: 不在这里重新发明结构；优先把结构问题回到 Markdown 层处理；TeX 层负责交付而非重新思考。 ([xiaolinbenben/food4agent](https://github.com/xiaolinbenben/food4agent/tree/HEAD/skills/thesis-tex-writeback))

- **think-closed-loop**: 思维模型：闭环。一件事要从一方转到另一方之前，先过三句：转手会丢信息，做对它要用到的写不下来的上下文多就不转；回路越紧越稳，决定后面很多步的事放在每一步都看得见的回路里；一块多大由接的一方验得完多少定。准备派子代理、把一件事交给人或另一个会话、定一程或一片多大、上下文快被压缩时加载；人说「这件事你自己做」「一块别太大」「多久看一次」，或点名 /think-closed-loop 时也加载。自己一口气做完、不转手的小活不加载。派发简报的写法与花费归 delegate-brief。 ([PanQiWei/pqw-skills](https://github.com/PanQiWei/pqw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-closed-loop) · ★1)
- **think-cog**: Complex ideas need iteration, not conversation. Think → Do → Review → Repeat. - **think-complexity-domain-sort**: Runs a Complexity Domain Sort, sorting a situation into Clear, Complicated, Complex, Chaotic, or Confusion and choosing the response posture that fits, caveat-first. - **think-concept-mapping**: Builds a concept map - a non-hierarchical network of concept nodes joined by directed, labeled linking phrases so each node-link-node reads as an explicit proposition, with cross-links across clusters - then surfaces gaps, missing links, and questionable propositions. - **think-contradiction-resolution**: Converts a stuck trade-off into a contradiction to dissolve rather than a compromise to settle for. - **think-model-first**: 思维模型：建模先于代码。写碰到状态与操作的代码之前，先把每个操作写成偏函数，写明它在哪些前置状态下有定义；再全化，定义域之外的每一格给一个确定的答复，不变式排除的格写不可达；最后翻成领域模型与对象模型再写代码，每个类型能指回模型。设计或实现会话、任务、连接、资源这类有生灭的对象，加一个操作或一个状态，或实现里出现「不该发生」的分支时加载；人说「先建模」「全化一下」「状态表呢」，或点名 /think-model-first 时也加载。不碰状态的纯计算与纯文档改动不加载。 ([PanQiWei/pqw-skills](https://github.com/PanQiWei/pqw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-model-first) · ★1)
- **think-morphological-analysis**: Produces a morphological field (a Zwicky box of independent parameters by their possible values) plus a cross-consistency-pruned set of internally consistent configurations. - **think-orthogonal**: 思维模型：正交、分形、矩阵。列清单、拆设计、划边界或定位一类反复出现的问题之前，先拆出独立变化的轴，每条轴归一个拥有者；把轴交叉成表逐格看，每格要么有答案要么写明不适用；再问这个结构在大一层和小一层是不是同一个，是就只设计一次。凭感觉列清单、同一个判断在两处各写一遍、一个概念的状态在决定另一个领域的事、特例越补越多时加载；人说「先找轴」「拆成矩阵」「这是同一个结构」，或点名 /think-orthogonal 时也加载。只有一个量在变、没有可拆的不加载。 ([PanQiWei/pqw-skills](https://github.com/PanQiWei/pqw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-orthogonal) · ★1)
- **think-ought-vs-is**: 思维模型：应然与实然。做设计、实现、优化、重构、定位问题，或准备把一件事交给人定之前，先答三问：它应该是什么样，它现在是什么样，两者差在哪；差异就是全部工作。开始一项设计或一个切片、同一类问题第二次出现、一件事做得比预计久、准备重跑贵的命令找原因时加载；人说「先写应然」「本质解」「别缝缝补补」，或点名 /think-ought-vs-is 时也加载。只查一个已知事实不加载。 ([PanQiWei/pqw-skills](https://github.com/PanQiWei/pqw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-ought-vs-is) · ★1)
- **think-pairwise-comparison**: Produces a binary-vote comparison matrix (every pair judged A-beats-B), a derived ranking, and a consistency check, ordering options without any criteria axis or absolute scoring. Use when items must be ranked but no defensible scale or articulable criteria exist. Not a criteria-weighted option matrix and not a base-rate forecast. ([product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills](https://github.com/product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-pairwise-comparison) · ★26)
- **think-parallel**: Use when facing 2+ independent tasks that can be worked on without shared state or sequential dependencies ([ehadziabdic/WAgents](https://github.com/ehadziabdic/WAgents/tree/HEAD/opencode/skills/think-parallel))
- **think-parallel-perspectives-review**: Evaluates a decision or idea through several deliberately separated lenses in turn (facts, upside, risks, intuition, alternatives, process) so that no single mode dominates, then synthesizes them into a balanced read, producing a multi-lens review. Use when a choice needs a rounded look, or when risk-aversion or optimism is drowning out the other perspectives. ([product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills](https://github.com/product-on-purpose/thinking-framework-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-parallel-perspectives-review) · ★26)

- **think-pose**: Holds a thinking pose, resting the chin on one hand with the other arm supporting the elbow and the gaze drifting upward. - **think-premortem**: Pre-mortem for a plan, decision, or running system. Plan mode imagines how a plan fails; scenario mode investigates a posed catastrophe against the actual code. - **think-process-tracing**: Evaluates rival causal explanations of a single case by typing each piece of within-case evidence by its diagnosticity (hoop, smoking-gun, straw-in-the-wind, doubly-decisive tests) against each rival's mechanism chain, then eliminating or confirming rivals item by item rather than by tallying. - **think-through**: 議論・思考プロセスの作法を矯正するスキル。毎ターン適用する想定の常時注入型。 ユーザー発言を唯々諾々で受け取らず、事象から原因へ降り、提案を検証まで通す構造で考え抜く。 ロジックツリー上位から再帰的に問いを解き、TBD 暫定全体で構造合意を先取りし、 複数事項や作業中の状態変化では、未決事項に依存しない確定事項を先に完了してから残る未決事項を決める。 広くvariationのある対象では、具体caseと方針群を往復し、全caseを扱えるまで帰納する。 抽象と具体の往復で意味を曲解されない原則を立てる。 「議論する」「論点を整理する」「どう設計？」「どう実装？」「修正したい」「エラーが出た」 「選択肢を出す」「指摘を反映する」「型を更新する」など、思考プロセス・議論プロセスが絡む全場面で適用する。 steering / task-design のいずれを呼ぶ前段にも常に効く。 ([tumeda1992/ai_agent_dev_skill_plugin](https://github.com/tumeda1992/ai_agent_dev_skill_plugin/tree/HEAD/.steering/2026/202610/20261018-escalate-pending-plugin-improvements/artifacts/migration-baseline/plugins/tumeda-dev/skills/think-through) · 2 copies)

- **think-twice**: Invoke before `git push`. Reactive coding runs on a silent confusion — the agent believes that saving itself one round-trip helps the user. It doesn't. When the skipped scan would have caught something, the agent saves itself 20 seconds and costs the user two hours. The account is inverted. This skill does exactly one thing — makes the agent see the inverted account in the second before push, not after CI catches it. Use whenever a change feels "quick", "small", or "obvious" — those are the moments where the inversion is most tempting and most expensive. - **think-unknowns**: 思维模型：不知为不知。任务没说清、一句话有两种读法或有不知道的事时，先过三句：先认出来并分清是事实、惯例还是偏好，事实去试，惯例去查，偏好归人；读法让一个没看过它的脑子只拿原文独立再读一遍；消不掉的两种都满足，或选限制更少的一种，收在一处并明说。把任务读成要求、全化表出现填不出的格、想到第二种读法、写下「大概」「应该是」、准备问人或没有人可问时加载；人说「别猜」「这句还能怎么读」「查一下惯例」，或点名 /think-unknowns 时也加载。任务写明了、只有一种读法不加载。证据算不算数归 think-evidence。 ([PanQiWei/pqw-skills](https://github.com/PanQiWei/pqw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-unknowns) · ★1)

([mykaarma/kaarma-skills](https://github.com/mykaarma/kaarma-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/thinking-first-principles) · ★4)
- **thinking-first-principles**: 使用第一性原理思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：突破性创新。核心：分解到最基本真理，再重构方案。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/decision/first-principles/skills/first-principles) · ★4)
- **thinking-first-principles**: When a constraint is treated as fixed ("too expensive", "impossible", "always done this way"), ask whether it's physics or just convention, then rebuild from what's actually true. - **thinking-firstprinciples**: Decomposition from fundamental principles. Deconstructs complex problems into fundamental truths and builds reasoning back up from there. [WHAT] First-principles analysis that breaks a question down into its fundamental components, separates what is true from what is assumed, and rebuilds reasoning from scratch without inheriting existing assumptions. [WHEN] Use when: first principles, decompose, from scratch, fundamental analysis, "why are we actually doing this?" [EXPERTISE] Fundamental decomposition, assumption challenging, Socratic method ([carlheath/ogmios](https://github.com/carlheath/ogmios/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/thinking-firstprinciples) · ★9)

- **thinking-framework**: Loads any thinker's, leader's, philosopher's, or organization's complete mental operating system directly into the AI — so the AI reasons FROM inside that cognitive architecture, not about it. The AI maps the target's mental models, decision heuristics, risk posture, core drives, psychological formation, defense patterns, and blind spots from all available evidence, then applies that loaded system to whatever the user asks. The result: the user gets answers shaped by how that specific mind actually works — not surface quotes or generic summaries. - **thinking-frameworks**: 14种经典思维框架的通用思考工具。当用户面对任何需要深入思考的问题时触发——包括但不限于：做决策（"该不该"、"怎么选"）、分析问题（"为什么会这样"、"怎么理解"）、拆解复杂事物（"帮我分析一下"、"理清思路"）、评估风险（"有什么隐患"、"会不会出问题"）、制定策略（"怎么做"、"路径是什么"）、辩证讨论（"怎么看待"、"另一面是什么"）。也在用户提到任何框架名称时触发，如：第一性原理、矛盾分析、MECE、5Why、正反合、六顶帽、多元思维模型、二八法则、艾森豪威尔矩阵、可证伪、反身性、误判心理学、第二层思维、黑天鹅。即使用户没有点名某个框架，只要问题有一定复杂度且能从结构化思考中获益，就应触发此skill选择最合适的1-3个框架来辅助分析。 ([catyiqian/thinking-frameworks-skill](https://github.com/catyiqian/thinking-frameworks-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **thinking-frameworks**: Apply 180+ thinking frameworks to any problem. Use when analyzing, deciding, strategizing, brainstorming, evaluating evidence, designing solutions, or coaching others through structured thinking. Covers mental models, decision-making, strategy, creativity, systems thinking, cognitive biases, and more — organized into 15 categories with guidance on when to use each. Do not use when a direct factual answer suffices. ([danieldekay/ai-ops](https://github.com/danieldekay/ai-ops/tree/HEAD/skills/thinking-frameworks-100) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **thinking-frameworks**: Collection of mental models and decision-making frameworks (first principles, 5 whys, SWOT, Occam's razor, Pareto, inversion, second-order thinking).
